— — —I—k—i— —š—i—o—l— —D—I— —m—o—d—e—l—i—ų— —k—ū—r—i—m—a—s— —r—ė—m—ė—s—i— —ž—m—o—n—i—ų— —p—a—r—a—š—y—t—a—i—s— —t—e—k—s—t—a—i—s— —i—r— —k—o—d—u— —i—š— —„—G—i—t—H—u—b—“— —a—r— —„—S—t—a—c—k— —O—v—e—r—f—l—o—w—“—.— —T—a—č—i—a—u— —š—i—e— —d—u—o—m—e—n—y—s— —b—a—i—g—ė—s—i—.— —N—a—u—j—a—s—i—s— —p—r—o—v—e—r—ž—i—o— —e—t—a—p—a—s—,— —a—p—i—e— —k—u—r—į— —l—a—i—d—o—j—e— —<—e—m—>—T—h—e— —D—i—a—r—y— —O—f— —A— —C—E—O—<—/—e—m—>— —k—a—l—b—ė—j—o— —e—k—s—p—e—r—t—a—i—,— —r—e—m—i—a—s—i— —d—v—i—e—m— —p—o—l—i—a—i—s—:— —<—s—t—r—o—n—g—>—s—i—n—t—e—t—i—n—i—a—i—s— —d—u—o—m—e—n—i—m—i—s—<—/—s—t—r—o—n—g—>— —i—r— —<—s—t—r—o—n—g—>—m—o—d—e—l—i—ų— —r—e—f—l—e—k—s—i—j—a— —a—t—l—i—e—k—a—n—t— —k—o—d—o— —o—p—t—i—m—i—z—a—v—i—m—ą—<—/—s—t—r—o—n—g—>—.—
